namespace = cosmo_fp_events

#upgrade palace
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_fp_events.1
	title = cosmo_fp_events.1.t
	desc = cosmo_fp_events.1.d
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {
	check_variable = {
			which = cosmoPalaceCounter	
			value = 1
			}
		}
			}	
	picture = cosmogfx_MNG_CONCUBINES_INTRODUCTION_eventPicture
	}
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {	
	check_variable = {
			which = cosmoPalaceCounter
			value = 21
			}
			}
		}	
	picture = cosmogfx_MNG_CONCUBINES_VISIT_1C_eventPicture
	}	
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {	
	check_variable = {
			which = cosmoPalaceCounter
			value = 41
			}
		}
		}	
	picture = cosmogfx_MNG_CONCUBINES_VISIT_1D_eventPicture
	}
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {	
	check_variable = {
			which = cosmoPalaceCounter
			value = 61
			}
		}
		}	
	picture = cosmogfx_MNG_CONCUBINES_VISIT_1E_eventPicture
	}
	picture = {
	trigger = {
		NOT = {
	check_variable = {
			which = cosmoPalaceCounter
			value = 81
			}
		}
	}	
	picture = cosmogfx_MNG_CONCUBINES_VISIT_1F_eventPicture
	}
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {	
	check_variable = {
			which = cosmoPalaceCounter
			value = 101
			}
	}	
	}
	picture = cosmogfx_MNG_CONCUBINES_VISIT_1G_eventPicture
	}
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {	
	check_variable = {
			which = cosmoPalaceCounter
			value = 121
			}
	}	
	}
	picture = cosmogfx_MNG_CONCUBINES_VISIT_1H_eventPicture
	}
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {	
	check_variable = {
			which = cosmoPalaceCounter
			value = 141
			}
	}	
	}
	picture = cosmogfx_MNG_CONCUBINES_VISIT_1B_eventPicture
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	hidden = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
	}	


	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_fp_events.1.a	
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_palace_menu_1	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_palace_menu_1_upgrade
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoPalaceUpgrade
		value = 1
		}		
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_palace_menu_2	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_palace_menu_2_upgrade
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoPalaceUpgrade
		value = 1
		}		
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_palace_menu_3	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_palace_menu_3_upgrade
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoPalaceUpgrade
		value = 1
		}		
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_palace_menu_4	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_palace_menu_4_upgrade
		}	
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_palace_menu_5	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_palace_menu_5_upgrade
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoPalaceUpgrade
		value = 1
		}		
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_palace_menu_6	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_palace_menu_6_upgrade
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoPalaceUpgrade
		value = 1
		}		
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_palace_menu_7	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_palace_menu_7_upgrade
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoPalaceUpgrade
		value = 1
		}		
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_palace_menu_8	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_palace_menu_8_upgrade
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoPalaceUpgrade
		value = 1
		}
		}								
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_country_flag = cosmo_palace_menu_1_upgrade	
			has_country_flag = cosmo_palace_menu_2_upgrade	
			has_country_flag = cosmo_palace_menu_3_upgrade	
			has_country_flag = cosmo_palace_menu_4_upgrade	
			has_country_flag = cosmo_palace_menu_5_upgrade	
			has_country_flag = cosmo_palace_menu_6_upgrade	
			has_country_flag = cosmo_palace_menu_7_upgrade	
			has_country_flag = cosmo_palace_menu_8_upgrade	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_palace_menu_all_upgrade
		}
				clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_palace_menu_1
				clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_palace_menu_2
				clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_palace_menu_3
				clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_palace_menu_4
				clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_palace_menu_5
				clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_palace_menu_6
				clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_palace_menu_7
				clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_palace_menu_8						
		}					
}

#Start event from estate
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_fp_events.10
	title = cosmo_fp_events.10.t
	desc = cosmo_fp_events.10.d
	picture = cosmogfx_PALACE_MENU_1A_0_eventPicture

	hidden = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		NOT = {
		has_ruler_flag = visiting_concubines
	}
	has_ruler_flag = cosmo_palace_launch
		
		}

		mean_time_to_happen = {
			days = 1
		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		set_ruler_flag = visiting_concubines
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_palace_launch
		remove_estate_privilege= estate_forbiddenpalace_ruler_visit
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_visit_concubines
			}
			random_list = {
				33 = { #adm
						set_ruler_flag = ruler_palace_adm
				}
				33 = { #dip
						set_ruler_flag = ruler_palace_dip
	
				}
				33 = { #mil
						set_ruler_flag = ruler_palace_mil
	
				}																				
			}
		}		
		}
		}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_fp_events.10.a
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_fp_events.30
			}																										
	}					
}

#grant palace 1a
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_fp_events.11
	title = cosmo_fp_events.11.t
	desc = cosmo_fp_events.11.d
	picture = cosmogfx_PALACE_MENU_1A_0_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = china_concubine_1a
		}
		}
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = xibei
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = xibei
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = xibei
				}
		}
			
	}

		mean_time_to_happen = {
			days = 1
		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = china_concubine_1a
			}
			remove_province_modifier = china_concubine_1a
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_concubines_in_capital
		if = {
			limit = {
			any_province = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = xibei
		}	
		}	
		set_ruler_flag = xibei_consort
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = xibei_consort	
		}	
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = xibei
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = xibei
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = xibei
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}				
		}
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = china_concubine_1a
				duration =  -1
				desc = china_concubine_1a_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_fp_events.11.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = xibei
				}
					cosmo_asian_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = xibei_consort	
		}	
		define_consort = {
			culture = xibei
		}
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_palace_1a								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_chinamarriage_events.101
			}
		clr_ruler_flag = xibei_consort
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_fp_events.11.b
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		clr_ruler_flag = xibei_consort
		}					
}

#grant palace 1b
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_fp_events.12
	title = cosmo_fp_events.12.t
	desc = cosmo_fp_events.12.d
	picture = cosmogfx_PALACE_MENU_1B_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = china_concubine_1b
		}
		}
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = zhongyuan
				culture = hubei
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = zhongyuan
				culture = hubei
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = zhongyuan					
				primary_culture = hubei					
				}
		}
			
	}

		mean_time_to_happen = {
			days = 1
		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = china_concubine_1b
			}
			remove_province_modifier = china_concubine_1b
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_concubines_in_capital
		if = {
			limit = {
			any_province = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = zhongyuan
		}	
		}	
		set_ruler_flag = zhongyuan_consort
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			any_province = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = hubei
		}	
		}	
		set_ruler_flag = hubei_consort
		clr_ruler_flag = zhongyuan_consort		
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = zhongyuan_consort	
		}	
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = zhongyuan
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = zhongyuan
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = zhongyuan
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}				
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = hubei_consort	
		}	
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = hubei
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = hubei
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = hubei
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}				
		}								
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = china_concubine_1b
				duration =  -1
				desc = china_concubine_1b_DESC

				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_fp_events.12.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = zhongyuan					
				primary_culture = hubei					
				}
					cosmo_asian_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_forbidden_consorts
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = zhongyuan_consort	
		}	
		define_consort = {
			culture = zhongyuan
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = hubei_consort	
		}	
		define_consort = {
			culture = hubei
		}
		}								
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_palace_1b								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_chinamarriage_events.101
			}
		clr_ruler_flag = zhongyuan_consort
		clr_ruler_flag = hubei_consort
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_fp_events.12.b
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}								
		clr_ruler_flag = zhongyuan_consort	
		clr_ruler_flag = hubei_consort
		}

#grant palace 1c
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_fp_events.13
	title = cosmo_fp_events.13.t
	desc = cosmo_fp_events.13.d
	picture = cosmogfx_PALACE_MENU_1C_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = china_concubine_1c
		}
		}
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = wu

			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = wu

			}				
			}			
				primary_culture = wu					

		}
			
	}

		mean_time_to_happen = {
			days = 1
		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = china_concubine_1c
			}
			remove_province_modifier = china_concubine_1c
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_concubines_in_capital
		if = {
			limit = {
			any_province = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = wu
		}	
		}	
		set_ruler_flag = wu_consort
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = wu_consort	
		}	
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = wu
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = wu
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = wu
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}				
		}								
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = china_concubine_1c
				duration =  -1
				desc = china_concubine_1c_DESC

				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_fp_events.13.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				primary_culture = wu					

					cosmo_asian_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_forbidden_consorts
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = shandong_culture_consort	
		}	
		define_consort = {
			culture = shandong_culture
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = wu_consort	
		}	
		define_consort = {
			culture = wu
		}
		}								
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_palace_1c								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_chinamarriage_events.101
			}
		clr_ruler_flag = shandong_culture_consort
		clr_ruler_flag = wu_consort
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_fp_events.13.b
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}

		clr_ruler_flag = wu_consort
		}

			has_ruler_flag = cantonese_consort	
		}	
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = cantonese
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = cantonese
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = cantonese
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}				
		}						
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = china_concubine_2c
				duration =  -1
				desc = china_concubine_2c_DESC

				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_fp_events.23.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
					primary_culture = cantonese
					cosmo_asian_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_forbidden_consorts
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cantonese_consort	
		}	
		define_consort = {
			culture = cantonese
		}
		}						
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_palace_2c								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_chinamarriage_events.101
			}
		clr_ruler_flag = cantonese_consort
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_fp_events.23.b
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {

			duration = 3650

#grant palace 3a miao
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_fp_events.31
	title = cosmo_fp_events.31.t
	desc = cosmo_fp_events.31.d
	picture = cosmogfx_PALACE_MENU_3A_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = china_concubine_3a
		}
		}
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = miao
				
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = miao
				
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = miao
				
				}
		}
			
	}

		mean_time_to_happen = {
			days = 1
		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = china_concubine_3a
			}
			remove_province_modifier = china_concubine_3a
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_concubines_in_capital
		if = {
			limit = {
			any_province = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = miao
		}	
		}	
		set_ruler_flag = miao_consort
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = miao_consort	
		}	
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = miao
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = miao
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = miao
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}				
		}
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = china_concubine_3a
				duration =  -1
				desc = china_concubine_3a_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_fp_events.31.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = miao
				
				}
					cosmo_asian_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = miao_consort	
		}	
		define_consort = {
			culture = miao
		}
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_palace_3a								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_chinamarriage_events.101
			}
		clr_ruler_flag = miao_consort

			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace

#grant palace 4a
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_fp_events.41
	title = cosmo_fp_events.41.t
	desc = cosmo_fp_events.41.d
	picture = cosmogfx_PALACE_MENU_4A_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = china_concubine_4a
		}
		}
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = manchu
				primary_culture = manchu_new
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = manchu
				primary_culture = manchu_new

			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				culture = manchu
				primary_culture = manchu_new					
				}
		}
			
	}

		mean_time_to_happen = {
			days = 1
		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = china_concubine_4a
			}
			remove_province_modifier = china_concubine_4a
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_concubines_in_capital
		if = {
			limit = {
			any_province = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = manchu
		}	
		}	
		set_ruler_flag = manchu_consort
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			any_province = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = manchu_new
		}	
		}	
		set_ruler_flag = manchu_new_consort
		clr_ruler_flag = manchu_consort
		}								
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = manchu_consort	
		}	
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = manchu
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = manchu
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = manchu
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}				
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = manchu_new_consort	
		}	
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = manchu_new
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = manchu_new
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = manchu_new
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}				
		}								
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = china_concubine_4a
				duration =  -1
				desc = china_concubine_4a_DESC

				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_fp_events.41.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
					culture = manchu
					cosmo_asian_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_forbidden_consorts
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = manchu_consort	
		}	
		define_consort = {
			culture = manchu
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = manchu_new_consort	
		}	
		define_consort = {
			culture = manchu_new
		}
		}								
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_palace_4a								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_chinamarriage_events.101
			}
		clr_ruler_flag = manchu_consort
		clr_ruler_flag = manchu_new_consort
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_fp_events.41.b
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA

		add_estate_influence_modifier = {

			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10

		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}

#grant palace 4c
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_fp_events.43
	title = cosmo_fp_events.43.t
	desc = cosmo_fp_events.43.d
	picture = cosmogfx_PALACE_MENU_4C_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = china_concubine_4c
		}
		}
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture_group = korean_g
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture_group = korean_g
			}
			}				
			}
				OR = {
				culture_group = korean_g					
				}
		}
			
	}

		mean_time_to_happen = {
			days = 1
		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = china_concubine_4c
			}
			remove_province_modifier = china_concubine_4c
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_concubines_in_capital
		if = {
			limit = {
			any_province = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = korean
		}	
		}	
		set_ruler_flag = korean_consort
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = korean_consort	
		}	
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = korean
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = korean
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = korean
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}				
		}						
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = china_concubine_4c
				duration =  -1
				desc = china_concubine_4c_DESC

				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_fp_events.43.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
					culture_group = korean_g
					cosmo_asian_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_forbidden_consorts
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = korean_consort	
		}	
		define_consort = {
			culture = korean
		}
		}						
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_palace_4c								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_chinamarriage_events.101
			}
		clr_ruler_flag = korean_consort
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {

				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = vietnamese
				NOT = {
					has_province_modifier = china_concubine_11c
				}
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}				
		}								
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = china_concubine_5b
				duration =  -1
				desc = china_concubine_5b_DESC

				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_fp_events.5202.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = vietnamese					
				}
					cosmo_asian_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_forbidden_consorts
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = vietnamese_consort	
		}	
		define_consort = {
			culture = vietnamese
		}
		}								
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_palace_5b								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_chinamarriage_events.101
			}
		clr_ruler_flag = vietnamese_consort
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_fp_events.5202.b
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}								
		clr_ruler_flag = vietnamese_consort			
		}

			any_province = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT

			}
			}			
		}
		clr_ruler_flag = vietnamese_consort
			every_province = {
				limit = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					has_province_modifier = china_concubine_5b					
				}
				remove_province_modifier = china_concubine_5b
			}		
			country_event = {
				id = cosmo_fp_events.5203
					days = 7
					random = 60	
				}		
		}
		option = {#send her away
		name = cosmo_fp_events.5202.7
		clr_ruler_flag = vietnamese_consort
			every_province = {
				limit = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					has_province_modifier = china_concubine_5b					
				}
				remove_province_modifier = china_concubine_5b
			}
		}

#Lao Sort
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_fp_events.5301
	title = cosmo_fp_events.5301.t
	desc = cosmo_fp_events.5301.d
	picture = cosmogfx_PALACE_MENU_5C_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	hidden = yes
	
	trigger = {
		ai = no	
		has_country_flag = cosmo_lao_search
	}

		mean_time_to_happen = {
			days = 1
		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		clr_country_flag = cosmo_lao_search
		
	}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_fp_events.5301.a
		random_list = {
			90 = {#5c
				trigger = {
					NOT = {
					any_province = {
						country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
							has_province_modifier = china_concubine_5c
					}
					}
				}
				country_event = {
					id = cosmo_fp_events.5302
					}				
			}
			1 = {#12c
				trigger = {
					NOT = {
					any_province = {
						country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
							has_province_modifier = china_concubine_12c
					}
					}
				}
				country_event = {
					id = cosmo_fp_events.5303
					}				
			}			
		}
	}					
}

			any_province = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = lao
		}	
		}	
		set_ruler_flag = lao_consort
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = lao_consort	
		}	
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = lao
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = lao
				NOT = {
					has_province_modifier = china_concubine_5c
				}
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}		
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = lao
				NOT = {
					has_province_modifier = china_concubine_5c
				}
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = lao
				NOT = {
					has_province_modifier = china_concubine_5c
				}
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}				
		}								
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = china_concubine_12c
				duration =  -1
				desc = china_concubine_12c_DESC

				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_fp_events.5303.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = lao					
				}
					cosmo_asian_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_forbidden_consorts
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = lao_consort	
		}	
		define_consort = {
			culture = lao
		}
		}								
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_palace_12c								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_chinamarriage_events.101
			}
		clr_ruler_flag = lao_consort
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_fp_events.5303.b
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}								
		clr_ruler_flag = lao_consort			
		}		
		option = {#seek other
		name = cosmo_fp_events.5303.c
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
			any_province = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					has_province_modifier = china_concubine_5c
			}
			}			
		}
			every_province = {
				limit = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					has_province_modifier = china_concubine_12c					
				}
				remove_province_modifier = china_concubine_12c
			}		
		clr_ruler_flag = lao_consort
			country_event = {
				id = cosmo_fp_events.5302
				days = 7
				random = 60						
				}		
		}
		option = {#send her away
		name = cosmo_fp_events.5303.7
		clr_ruler_flag = lao_consort
			every_province = {
				limit = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					has_province_modifier = china_concubine_12c					
				}
				remove_province_modifier = china_concubine_12c
			}
		}									
}

country_event = {
	id = cosmo_fp_events.61
	title = cosmo_fp_events.61.t
	desc = cosmo_fp_events.61.d
	picture = cosmogfx_PALACE_MENU_6A_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = china_concubine_6a
		}
		}
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = shan
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = shan

			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = shan					
				}
		}
			
	}

		mean_time_to_happen = {
			days = 1
		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = china_concubine_6a
			}
			remove_province_modifier = china_concubine_6a
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_concubines_in_capital
		if = {
			limit = {
			any_province = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = shan
		}	
		}	
		set_ruler_flag = shan_consort
		}

			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = shan_consort

		}

			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = shan
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = shan
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}				
		}						
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes

				name = china_concubine_6a
				duration =  -1

				}			
			}																													
		}
	}

			}

		clr_ruler_flag = central_thai_consort
			every_province = {

#grant palace 12a
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_fp_events.6203
	title = cosmo_fp_events.6203.t
	desc = cosmo_fp_events.6203.d
	picture = cosmogfx_PALACE_MENU_12A_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		ai = no	
	}


	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = china_concubine_12a
			}
			remove_province_modifier = china_concubine_12a
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_concubines_in_capital
		if = {
			limit = {
			any_province = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = central_thai
		}	
		}	
		set_ruler_flag = central_thai_consort
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = central_thai_consort	
		}	
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = central_thai
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = central_thai
				NOT = {
					has_province_modifier = china_concubine_12b
					has_province_modifier = china_concubine_5c
				}
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}		
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = central_thai
				NOT = {
					has_province_modifier = china_concubine_12b
					has_province_modifier = china_concubine_5c
				}
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = central_thai
				NOT = {
					has_province_modifier = china_concubine_12b
					has_province_modifier = china_concubine_5c
				}
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}				
		}								
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = china_concubine_12a
				duration =  -1
				desc = china_concubine_12a_DESC

				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_fp_events.6203.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = central_thai					
				}
					cosmo_asian_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_forbidden_consorts
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = central_thai_consort	
		}	
		define_consort = {
			culture = central_thai
		}
		}								
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_palace_12a								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_chinamarriage_events.101
			}
		clr_ruler_flag = central_thai_consort
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_fp_events.6203.b
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}								
		clr_ruler_flag = central_thai_consort			
		}		
		option = {#seek other
		name = cosmo_fp_events.6203.c
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
			any_province = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					has_province_modifier = china_concubine_12b
			}
			}			
		}
			every_province = {
				limit = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					has_province_modifier = china_concubine_12a					
				}
				remove_province_modifier = china_concubine_12a
			}		
		clr_ruler_flag = central_thai_consort
			country_event = {
				id = cosmo_fp_events.6204
				days = 7
				random = 60						
				}		
		}
		option = {#send her away
		name = cosmo_fp_events.6203.7
		clr_ruler_flag = central_thai_consort
			every_province = {
				limit = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					has_province_modifier = china_concubine_12a					
				}
				remove_province_modifier = china_concubine_12a
			}
		}									
}

#Central Thai Sort
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_fp_events.6206
	title = cosmo_fp_events.6206.t
	desc = cosmo_fp_events.6206.d
	picture = cosmogfx_PALACE_MENU_13A_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	hidden = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no	
		has_country_flag = cosmo_northern_thai_search
	}

		mean_time_to_happen = {
			days = 1
		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		clr_country_flag = cosmo_northern_thai_search
		
	}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_fp_events.6206.a
		random_list = {
			1 = {#13a
				trigger = {
					NOT = {
					any_province = {
						country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
							has_province_modifier = china_concubine_13a
					}
					}
				}
				country_event = {
					id = cosmo_fp_events.6207
					}				
			}
			1 = {#13b
				trigger = {
					NOT = {
					any_province = {
						country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
							has_province_modifier = china_concubine_13b
					}
					}
				}
				country_event = {
					id = cosmo_fp_events.6208
					}				
			}
			1 = {#13c
				trigger = {
					NOT = {
					any_province = {
						country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
							has_province_modifier = china_concubine_13c
					}
					}
				}
				country_event = {
					id = cosmo_fp_events.6209
					}				
			}						
		}
	}

		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {

			influence = -10
			duration = 3650

			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA

#grant palace 13b
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_fp_events.6208
	title = cosmo_fp_events.6208.t
	desc = cosmo_fp_events.6208.d
	picture = cosmogfx_PALACE_MENU_13B_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		ai = no	
	}


	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = china_concubine_13b
			}
			remove_province_modifier = china_concubine_13b
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_concubines_in_capital
		if = {
			limit = {
			any_province = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = northern_thai
		}	
		}	
		set_ruler_flag = northern_thai_consort
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = northern_thai_consort	
		}	
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = northern_thai
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = northern_thai
				NOT = {
					has_province_modifier = china_concubine_13a
					has_province_modifier = china_concubine_13c
				}
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}		
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = northern_thai
				NOT = {
					has_province_modifier = china_concubine_13a
					has_province_modifier = china_concubine_13c
				}
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = northern_thai
				NOT = {
					has_province_modifier = china_concubine_13a
					has_province_modifier = china_concubine_13c
				}
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}				
		}								
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = china_concubine_13b
				duration =  -1
				desc = china_concubine_13b_DESC

				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_fp_events.6208.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = northern_thai					
				}
					cosmo_asian_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_forbidden_consorts
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = northern_thai_consort	
		}	
		define_consort = {
			culture = northern_thai
		}
		}								
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_palace_13b								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_chinamarriage_events.101
			}
		clr_ruler_flag = northern_thai_consort
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_fp_events.6208.b
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}								
		clr_ruler_flag = northern_thai_consort			
		}		
		option = {#seek other
		name = cosmo_fp_events.6208.c
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
			any_province = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					has_province_modifier = china_concubine_13c
			}
			}			
		}
			every_province = {
				limit = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					has_province_modifier = china_concubine_13b					
				}
				remove_province_modifier = china_concubine_13b
			}		
		clr_ruler_flag = northern_thai_consort
			country_event = {
				id = cosmo_fp_events.6209
				days = 7
				random = 60						
				}		
		}
		option = {#send her away
		name = cosmo_fp_events.6208.7
		clr_ruler_flag = northern_thai_consort
			every_province = {
				limit = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT

country_event = {
	id = cosmo_fp_events.63
	title = cosmo_fp_events.63.t
	desc = cosmo_fp_events.63.d
	picture = cosmogfx_PALACE_MENU_6C_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = china_concubine_6c
		}
		}
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = khmer
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = khmer
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = khmer					
				}
		}
			
	}

		mean_time_to_happen = {
			days = 1
		}

	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {

				has_province_modifier = china_concubine_6c
			}
			remove_province_modifier = china_concubine_6c

				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = khmer
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = khmer
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}				
		}						
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = china_concubine_6c
				duration =  -1
				desc = china_concubine_6c_DESC

				}			
			}																													
		}
	}

			}

					primary_culture = khmer
					cosmo_asian_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}

			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives

#grant palace 7a
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_fp_events.71
	title = cosmo_fp_events.71.t
	desc = cosmo_fp_events.71.d
	picture = cosmogfx_PALACE_MENU_7A_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = china_concubine_7a
		}
		}
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = sumatran
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = sumatran
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = sumatran					
				}
		}
			
	}

		mean_time_to_happen = {
			days = 1
		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = china_concubine_7a
			}
			remove_province_modifier = china_concubine_7a
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_concubines_in_capital
		if = {
			limit = {
			any_province = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = sumatran
		}	
		}	
		set_ruler_flag = sumatran_consort
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = sumatran_consort	
		}	
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = sumatran
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = sumatran
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = sumatran
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}				
		}						
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = china_concubine_7a
				duration =  -1
				desc = china_concubine_7a_DESC

				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_fp_events.71.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
					primary_culture = sumatran
					cosmo_asian_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_forbidden_consorts
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = sumatran_consort	
		}	
		define_consort = {
			culture = sumatran
		}
		}						
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_palace_7a								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_chinamarriage_events.101
			}
		clr_ruler_flag = sumatran_consort
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_fp_events.71.b
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}								
		clr_ruler_flag = sumatran_consort			
		}					
}

					}

		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = china_concubine_8b
		}
		}
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = filipino
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = filipino
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = filipino					
				}
		}
			
	}

		mean_time_to_happen = {
			days = 1
		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = china_concubine_8b
			}
			remove_province_modifier = china_concubine_8b
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_concubines_in_capital
		if = {
			limit = {
			any_province = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = filipino
		}	
		}	
		set_ruler_flag = filipino_consort
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = filipino_consort	
		}	
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = filipino
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = filipino
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = filipino
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}				
		}						
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = china_concubine_8b
				duration =  -1
				desc = china_concubine_8b_DESC

				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_fp_events.82.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
					primary_culture = filipino
					cosmo_asian_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_forbidden_consorts
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = filipino_consort	
		}	
		define_consort = {
			culture = filipino
		}
		}						
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_palace_8b								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_chinamarriage_events.101
			}
		clr_ruler_flag = filipino_consort
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_fp_events.82.b
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem

			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}

#grant palace 9c
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_fp_events.93
	title = cosmo_fp_events.93.t
	desc = cosmo_fp_events.93.d
	picture = cosmogfx_PALACE_MENU_9C_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = china_concubine_9c
		}
		}
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = hubei
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = hubei
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = hubei					
				}
		}
			
	}

		mean_time_to_happen = {
			days = 1
		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = china_concubine_9c
			}
			remove_province_modifier = china_concubine_9c
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_concubines_in_capital
		if = {
			limit = {
			any_province = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = hubei
		}	
		}	
		set_ruler_flag = hubei_consort
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = hubei_consort	
		}	
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = hubei
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = hubei
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = hubei
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}				
		}						
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = china_concubine_9c
				duration =  -1
				desc = china_concubine_9c_DESC

				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_fp_events.93.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
					primary_culture = hubei
					cosmo_asian_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_forbidden_consorts
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}

			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = hubei_consort	
		}	
		define_consort = {
			culture = hubei
		}
		}						
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_palace_9c
