#grant palace 10c
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_fp_events.1003
	title = cosmo_fp_events.1003.t
	desc = cosmo_fp_events.1003.d
	picture = cosmogfx_PALACE_MENU_10C_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = china_concubine_10c
		}
		}
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = zhuang
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = zhuang
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = zhuang					
				}
		}
			
	}

		mean_time_to_happen = {
			days = 1
		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = china_concubine_10c
			}
			remove_province_modifier = china_concubine_10c
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_concubines_in_capital
		if = {
			limit = {
			any_province = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = zhuang
		}	
		}	
		set_ruler_flag = zhuang_consort
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = zhuang_consort	
		}	
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = zhuang
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = zhuang
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = zhuang
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}				
		}						
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = china_concubine_10c
				duration =  -1
				desc = china_concubine_10c_DESC

				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_fp_events.1003.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
					primary_culture = zhuang
					cosmo_asian_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_forbidden_consorts
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = zhuang_consort	
		}	
		define_consort = {
			culture = zhuang
		}
		}						
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_palace_10c								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_chinamarriage_events.101
			}
		clr_ruler_flag = zhuang_consort
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_fp_events.1003.b
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {

			remove_province_modifier = china_concubine_15a
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_concubines_in_capital
		if = {
			limit = {
			any_province = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = gamilaraay
		}	
		}	
		set_ruler_flag = gamilaraay_consort
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = gamilaraay_consort	
		}	
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = gamilaraay
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = gamilaraay
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT

				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}				
		}

				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}

				name = china_concubine_15a
				duration =  -1
				desc = china_concubine_15a_DESC

				}			
			}																													
		}
	}

			}

				}
			}

		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_ooku

		}
		}

		}

			OR = {
				culture = togoku
				culture = japanese
				culture = kyushuan
			}
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
					superregion = east_indies_superregion
					province_id = 1015
			}
			OR = {
				culture = togoku
				culture = japanese
				culture = kyushuan
			}
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}				
		}								
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = china_concubine_14a
				duration =  -1
				desc = china_concubine_14a_DESC

				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_fp_events.1047.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_forbidden_consorts
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = japan_consort	
		}	
		define_consort = {
			culture = japanese
		}
		}								
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_palace_14a								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_chinamarriage_events.101
			}
		clr_ruler_flag = japan_consort
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_fp_events.1047.b
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}								
		clr_ruler_flag = japan_consort			
		}		
		option = {#seek other
		name = cosmo_fp_events.1047.c
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
			any_province = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					has_province_modifier = china_concubine_14b
			}
			}			
		}
		clr_ruler_flag = japan_consort
			every_province = {
				limit = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					has_province_modifier = china_concubine_14a					
				}
				remove_province_modifier = china_concubine_14a
			}		
			country_event = {
				id = cosmo_fp_events.1048
					days = 7
					random = 60	
				}		
		}
		option = {#send her away
		name = cosmo_fp_events.1047.7
		clr_ruler_flag = japan_consort
			every_province = {
				limit = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					has_province_modifier = china_concubine_14a					
				}
				remove_province_modifier = china_concubine_14a
			}
		}

#grant palace 14b
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_fp_events.1048
	title = cosmo_fp_events.1048.t
	desc = cosmo_fp_events.1048.d
	picture = cosmogfx_PALACE_MENU_14B_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		ai = no	
	}


	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = china_concubine_14b
			}
			remove_province_modifier = china_concubine_14b
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_concubines_in_capital
		if = {
			limit = {
			any_province = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
					superregion = east_indies_superregion
					province_id = 1015

			OR = {
				culture = togoku
				culture = japanese
				culture = kyushuan
			}
		}	
		}

		}

			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = japan_consort	
		}

			limit = {

		}

		}

#grant palace 17a yura
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_fp_events.1071
	title = cosmo_fp_events.1071.t
	desc = cosmo_fp_events.1071.d
	picture = cosmogfx_PALACE_MENU_17A_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = china_concubine_17a
		}
		}
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = yura
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = yura
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = yura					
				}
		}
			
	}

		mean_time_to_happen = {
			days = 1
		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = china_concubine_17a
			}
			remove_province_modifier = china_concubine_17a
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_concubines_in_capital
		if = {
			limit = {
			any_province = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = yura
		}	
		}	
		set_ruler_flag = yura_consort
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = yura_consort	
		}	
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = yura
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = yura
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = yura

			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}				
		}

				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes

			}

#grant palace 17b maori
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_fp_events.1072
	title = cosmo_fp_events.1072.t
	desc = cosmo_fp_events.1072.d
	picture = cosmogfx_PALACE_MENU_17B_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = china_concubine_17b
		}
		}
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = maori
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = maori
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = maori					
				}
		}
			
	}

		mean_time_to_happen = {
			days = 1
		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = china_concubine_17b
			}
			remove_province_modifier = china_concubine_17b
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_concubines_in_capital
		if = {
			limit = {
			any_province = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = maori
		}	
		}	
		set_ruler_flag = maori_consort
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = maori_consort	
		}	
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = maori
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = maori
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = maori
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}				
		}						
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = china_concubine_17b
				duration =  -1
				desc = china_concubine_17b_DESC

				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_fp_events.1072.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
					primary_culture = maori
					cosmo_asian_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_forbidden_consorts
		}

#grant palace 18b nyoongah
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_fp_events.1082
	title = cosmo_fp_events.1082.t
	desc = cosmo_fp_events.1082.d
	picture = cosmogfx_PALACE_MENU_18B_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = china_concubine_18b
		}
		}
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = nyoongah
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = nyoongah
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = nyoongah					
				}
		}
			
	}

		mean_time_to_happen = {
			days = 1
		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = china_concubine_18b
			}
			remove_province_modifier = china_concubine_18b
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_concubines_in_capital
		if = {
			limit = {
			any_province = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = nyoongah
		}	
		}	
		set_ruler_flag = nyoongah_consort
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = nyoongah_consort	
		}	
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = nyoongah
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = nyoongah
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = nyoongah
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}				
		}						
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = china_concubine_18b
				duration =  -1
				desc = china_concubine_18b_DESC

				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_fp_events.1082.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
					primary_culture = nyoongah
					cosmo_asian_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_forbidden_consorts
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = nyoongah_consort	
		}	
		define_consort = {
			culture = nyoongah
		}
		}						
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_palace_18b								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_chinamarriage_events.101
			}
		clr_ruler_flag = nyoongah_consort
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_fp_events.1082.b
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}								
		clr_ruler_flag = nyoongah_consort			
		}					
}

		}
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = aboriginal
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = aboriginal
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = aboriginal					
				}
		}
			
	}

		mean_time_to_happen = {
			days = 1
		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = china_concubine_19b
			}
			remove_province_modifier = china_concubine_19b
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_concubines_in_capital
		if = {
			limit = {
			any_province = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = aboriginal
		}	
		}	
		set_ruler_flag = aboriginal_consort
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = aboriginal_consort	
		}	
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = aboriginal
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = aboriginal
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = aboriginal
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}				
		}						
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = china_concubine_19b
				duration =  -1
				desc = china_concubine_19b_DESC

				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_fp_events.1092.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
					primary_culture = aboriginal
					cosmo_asian_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_forbidden_consorts
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {

			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = aboriginal_consort	
		}	
		define_consort = {
			culture = aboriginal
		}
		}						
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_palace_19b								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_chinamarriage_events.101
			}
		clr_ruler_flag = aboriginal_consort
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {

			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {

			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}

			duration = 3650

			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}

#grant palace 20c hubei
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_fp_events.2003
	title = cosmo_fp_events.2003.t
	desc = cosmo_fp_events.2003.d
	picture = cosmogfx_PALACE_MENU_20C_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = china_concubine_20c
		}
		}
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = hubei
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = hubei
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = hubei					
				}
		}
			
	}

		mean_time_to_happen = {
			days = 1
		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = china_concubine_20c
			}
			remove_province_modifier = china_concubine_20c
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_concubines_in_capital
		if = {
			limit = {
			any_province = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = hubei
		}	
		}	
		set_ruler_flag = hubei_consort
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = hubei_consort	
		}	
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = hubei
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = hubei
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = hubei
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}				
		}						
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = china_concubine_20c
				duration =  -1
				desc = china_concubine_20c_DESC

				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_fp_events.2003.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
					primary_culture = hubei
					cosmo_asian_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_forbidden_consorts
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = hubei_consort	
		}	
		define_consort = {
			culture = hubei
		}
		}						
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_palace_20c								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_chinamarriage_events.101
			}
		clr_ruler_flag = hubei_consort
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_fp_events.2003.b
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650

		add_estate_influence_modifier = {

#grant palace 21c hakka
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_fp_events.2013
	title = cosmo_fp_events.2013.t
	desc = cosmo_fp_events.2013.d
	picture = cosmogfx_PALACE_MENU_21C_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = china_concubine_21c
		}
		}
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = hakka
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = hakka
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = hakka					
				}
		}
			
	}

		mean_time_to_happen = {
			days = 1
		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = china_concubine_21c
			}
			remove_province_modifier = china_concubine_21c
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_concubines_in_capital
		if = {
			limit = {
			any_province = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = hakka
		}	
		}	
		set_ruler_flag = hakka_consort
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = hakka_consort	
		}	
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = hakka
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = hakka
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = hakka
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}				
		}						
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = china_concubine_21c
				duration =  -1
				desc = china_concubine_21c_DESC

				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_fp_events.2013.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
					primary_culture = hakka
					cosmo_asian_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_forbidden_consorts
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = hakka_consort	
		}	
		define_consort = {
			culture = hakka
		}
		}						
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_palace_21c								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_chinamarriage_events.101
			}
		clr_ruler_flag = hakka_consort
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_fp_events.2013.b
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}								
		clr_ruler_flag = hakka_consort			
		}					
}

		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = evenk
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = evenk
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = evenk
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}				
		}						
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = china_concubine_22c
				duration =  -1
				desc = china_concubine_22c_DESC

				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_fp_events.2023.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
					primary_culture = evenk
					cosmo_asian_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_forbidden_consorts
		}

			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = evenk_consort	
		}	
		define_consort = {
			culture = evenk
		}
		}						
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_palace_22c								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_chinamarriage_events.101
			}
		clr_ruler_flag = evenk_consort
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650

			duration = 3650

			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}

#grant palace 23a khazak
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_fp_events.2031
	title = cosmo_fp_events.2031.t
	desc = cosmo_fp_events.2031.d
	picture = cosmogfx_PALACE_MENU_23A_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = china_concubine_23a
		}
		}
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = khazak
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = khazak
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = khazak					
				}
		}
			
	}

		mean_time_to_happen = {
			days = 1
		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = china_concubine_23a
			}
			remove_province_modifier = china_concubine_23a
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_concubines_in_capital
		if = {
			limit = {
			any_province = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = khazak
		}	
		}	
		set_ruler_flag = khazak_consort
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = khazak_consort	
		}	
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = khazak
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = khazak
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = khazak
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}				
		}						
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = china_concubine_23a
				duration =  -1
				desc = china_concubine_23a_DESC

				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_fp_events.2031.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
					primary_culture = khazak
					cosmo_asian_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_forbidden_consorts
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = khazak_consort	
		}	
		define_consort = {
			culture = khazak
		}
		}						
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_palace_23a								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_chinamarriage_events.101
			}

#grant palace 24a khalkha
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_fp_events.2041
	title = cosmo_fp_events.2041.t
	desc = cosmo_fp_events.2041.d
	picture = cosmogfx_PALACE_MENU_24A_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = china_concubine_24a
		}
		}
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = khalkha
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 3
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = khalkha
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = khalkha					
				}
		}
			
	}

		mean_time_to_happen = {
			days = 1
		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = china_concubine_24a
			}
			remove_province_modifier = china_concubine_24a
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_concubines_in_capital
		if = {
			limit = {
			any_province = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = khalkha
		}	
		}	
		set_ruler_flag = khalkha_consort
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = khalkha_consort	
		}	
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = khalkha
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = khalkha
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = khalkha
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}				
		}						
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = china_concubine_24a
				duration =  -1
				desc = china_concubine_24a_DESC

				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_fp_events.2041.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
					primary_culture = khalkha
					cosmo_asian_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_forbidden_consorts
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = khalkha_consort	
		}	
		define_consort = {
			culture = khalkha
		}
		}						
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_palace_24a								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_chinamarriage_events.101
			}
		clr_ruler_flag = khalkha_consort
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_fp_events.2041.b
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONCUBINE_CHINA

			duration = 3650
		}

country_event = {
	id = cosmo_fp_events.30
	title = cosmo_fp_events.30.t
	desc = cosmo_fp_events.30.d
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {
	check_variable = {
			which = cosmoPalaceCounter	
			value = 1
			}
		}
			}	
	picture = cosmogfx_MNG_CONCUBINES_INTRODUCTION_eventPicture
	}

				limit = {
				capital_scope = {
					region = malaya_region
				}
				}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_fp_events.700
			}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
				capital_scope = {
				OR = {
					region = moluccas_region
					region = indonesia_region
				}
				}
				}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_fp_events.800
			}
			}
		}			
}
